# CLAUDE.md — wuld.ink project primer

You are a Cowork instance working on Josiah's `wuld.ink` personal site. Read this file first. Then read `docs/wuld-ink-cowork-brief.md`. That is the orientation sequence.

---

## What this project is

A multi-page philosophical-content site for Josiah (WULD / AnomicIndividual87 / Evilis Anihilis Uls). Cloudflare end-to-end stack (Registrar + Pages + R2). Domain `wuld.ink` registered 2026-05-11 (auto-renew $20/yr, expires 2027-05-11). The site is the umbrella for all his philosophical output.

---

## Orientation sequence (in order)

1. Read this file.
2. Read `docs/wuld-ink-cowork-brief.md` — full implementation brief, stack rationale, capacity math, audio architecture, open decisions, working preferences.
3. Confirm what Josiah is asking. If a session-A-prerequisite decision is still open (see "Current state" below), surface it via `AskUserQuestion` before building anything.

JS sets `aria-current="page"` on matching link (session B). **K43:** nav-glow — `.nav-updated` text-shadow pulse on nav items whose section changed since the visitor's last visit (localStorage `wuld:seen` diffed against `/releases.json`); cleared on visiting that section; `prefers-reduced-motion` -> steady accent, no pulse. |

| `src/components/ambient-player.{css,js}` | Site-wide ambient background player (K24j). Fixed-bottom 2.5rem mono chrome bar (track name + play/pause + skip + shuffle + volume + ambient on/off). Hidden YouTube IFrame loaded from youtube-nocookie.com pulling playlist `PLt28yN-6sGYFrlBca9RI70IjQ2ny50D1c`. localStorage key `wuld:ambient` persists `{on, volume, currentVideoId, lastPositionSec, shuffleOn}` across navigation. Browser autoplay-policy hard-block worked around via first-interaction listener (click/touch/keydown) bound when initial `playVideo()` is silently denied; `.ambient-needs-tap` pulse class signals the play button until interaction unsticks autoplay. Cross-page seek-resume via saved `currentVideoId` + `lastPositionSec` (re-found in next page's playlist; small delay before `seekTo`). Skipped surfaces: `/_/successor-protocol/` + `glossary/black-box-of-inaccessibility/` + `404.html` + both templates. **K207:** `DEFAULT_STATE.shuffleOn` true->false + `loopOne` false->true -> a fresh visitor opens on playlist track 1, looped (the paired opening track for the wrong-hour synth bed; existing listeners keep their persisted shuffle/loop); js `?v=K30`->`?v=K207` (css HELD `?v=K30`). |
| `src/components/wrong-hour.{css,js}` | Synthesized SFX/VFX component ("the wrong hour"), K205; NO-PIN auto-deploying, progressive (JS off -> zero effects; homepage D1 zero-JS invariant holds). ONE DIAL `rot() = prefs.vfx x (0.4 + 0.6*bleedNow())`, the bleed peaking ~3am; drives colour-tint (mix-blend:color, LIGHTNESS held) + real canvas film-grain + scanlines + heading chromatic-aberration + vignette on a 60s paint tick. Synth CUE LIBRARY (boot / key-per-keystroke / soft-nav / click-button / bell-3am / purr-mascot), consent-gated, lazy AudioContext resumed on our OWN passive first-gesture listener (coexists with the ambient-player YouTube unlock, never touches it); placement router nav->soft / buttons->click / typing->key + `data-wh` ("none" mutes) / `data-wh-scene` / `data-wh-hover`. Controls dock into `.ambient-bar` ([fx] chip -> popover); localStorage `wuld:wrongHour`; prefers-reduced-motion -> motion off. **K206:** sfx default 0->0.35; synced-smart boot power-on (flash fires WITH the boot sound on the first gesture, or immediately if sound muted); GENERATIVE AMBIANCE ENGINE (mechanical whir/hum drone + sparse analog-piano motifs + faint breath through the convolver room) as a `youtube <-> generated` source toggle on the bar + a "bed" popover row (pauses the playlist via `WuldAmbient`, rides the ambient volume, persists via `prefs.amb`; NO ambient-player.js edit); VFX REGISTRY of interaction one-shots (flick/pulse/burst/roll/bloom + heading `glitch`) routed parallel to the sound cues (nav->pulse / buttons->flick / typing->glitch + `data-wh-vfx` / `data-wh-vfx-scene`), peaks swelled by the same bleed (`fxi()`), legibility-safe, off under reduced-motion. Public `window.WuldWrongHour` (play/fx/bed/get/set + cues/vfx lists). **K207:** the bed DECOUPLED from YouTube (independent layer -- never pauses the playlist, fixing the K206 dead-bar bug: pausing YT flipped `[data-state=off]` -> `pointer-events:none` on the injected chips) + bed ON by default (`prefs.bedOn`) + auto-disable on YT track-change/skip (a 2s `WuldAmbient.currentVideoId` watch) + a resonant-pentatonic-pluck nav cue (`soft`, replacing the felt-tap); `ambience`->`bed` API; a css override keeps `[fx]`/`[synth bed]` live at `[data-state=off]`. `?v=K207`. **K208:** a notes-surface `word()`/`wordChime` cue (soft high-pentatonic "word landed" chime) + `setWriting()`/`ambFlourish()` (writing-mode bed reactivity); a SESSION-AGE STRANGENESS engine -- `corruption()` ramps 0->1 over ~30min of tab-open time (sessionStorage `wuld:wrongHour.sess`, resets on browser close) driving intrusive-word hover ghosts + reversible single-text-node glyph flicker + phantom link-destination labels (gated `vfx>0 && !reduce`, one throttle, NEVER mutates the editor/real nav, excludes textarea/input/contenteditable/`[data-wh=none]`/ambient-bar/panel); TWO additive bed MOODS `clinical`+`oceanic` via a `MOODS[bedMood]` config + a `[bed: <mood>]` popover cycle (`prefs.bedMood`). Public surface += `word/writing/mood/corr/moods`; `?v=K207`->`?v=K208` (js `b32b3825`->`0fa0932a`/865 ln, css `d101e59b`->`263f54a7`/202 ln). |

---

## Scope: what Cowork is for vs not for

### Cowork is FOR (most build work):

- Design system: typography scale, color tokens, spacing rhythm, component library
- HTML/CSS/JS implementation across all pages
- Audio player component + `data-audio-key` wiring to R2
- Build tooling, GitHub repo init, Cloudflare Pages config, DNSSEC enable
- Iterative debug/fix/refine cycles
- File reorganization, find-and-replace, structural lookups
- R2 bucket setup, custom subdomain (`audio.wuld.ink`) config

### Cowork is NOT FOR (defer to chat):

- Drafting essay or blog **content** (philosophical writing)
- Generating glossary entries (vocabulary curation)
- Drafting Void Engine prompts (semantic content authorship)
- Major architectural pivots (re-locking the stack, swapping registrar)
- Anything that would update the **efilist** project's canon (different project) — **EXCEPTION (ratified K48-close, 2026-06-01):** rebuttal-strengthen foldins that *ship to live + need a wuld.ink pin move* are routed to wuld.ink Cowork **end-to-end**. The deploy tail (`verify-live-library.ps1` + `tools/library-pin.py --apply`) is wuld.ink-exclusive, so splitting push-from-pin across projects creates the seam that stalls the chain; one project end-to-end = atomic. Corpus-internal / no-pin work (compaction, validator runs, schema migration) stays with the Argument Library project; MAX authoring (philosophy, cold-grade) stays in-chat. One-deliverable-per-session still holds.

Rule of thumb: **Cowork builds the vessel; chat fills it with content.**

---

## Register and working norms

- **Direct.** No hedging, apologies, sycophancy, "great question" openers.
- **Iconoclastic framing welcome** where it earns its keep.
- **Disagree substantively** — don't flatter, push back where the user's logic has gaps. Concede only to superior argument.
- **No menus.** Recommendation first, then supporting reasoning.
- **Concise.** Josiah reads without prescription glasses currently — keep blocks short, scannable.
- **Pre-flag token-budget risks.** Multi-session arc; sessions that may hit ceilings mid-build should be re-scoped, not pushed through.

---

## Aesthetic register (LOCKED — applies to all visual decisions)

- **Mode:** Neobrutalist dark-mode (canonical). Reader-mode (warm-cream light) and high-contrast (HC) modes are SCOPED accessibility affordances on heavy-read containers only — not site-global theme switches.
- **Typography (book-matched):** Three serif faces mirroring Josiah's book + IBM Plex Mono retained for UI chrome only.
  - **Cormorant Garamond** (Christian Thalmann) — `--font-display`: homepage hero, title-page, book-cover register.
  - **IM Fell English** (Igino Marini) — `--font-headline`: h1, h2 essay/section titles.
  - **EB Garamond** (Georg Duffner) — `--font-body`: body text, h3-h5 subheadings.
  - **IBM Plex Mono** — `--font-mono`: eyebrow labels, audio readouts, mode-toggle buttons, code, metadata, footer, nav. The "manufactured object" signal (running heads, colophons, page numbers in literary-press parlance). **Also the cross-surface anchor** between wuld.ink (where Mono = chrome) and `library.wuld.ink` (where Mono = everywhere, the instrument-panel diegetic skin). The Mono spine is the typographic constant across the umbrella; non-chrome typography diverges by design (serif on wuld.ink for content register; mono throughout on library for instrument register).
  - All three serifs self-hosted via `@font-face` with `local()` first in the src chain (renders Josiah's installed copies immediately, falls through to WOFF2 in `src/fonts/` for web visitors).
  - Root font-size bumped to 18px (`html { font-size: 112.5% }`) to compensate for Garamond's small x-height — also serves Josiah's no-glasses reading constraint.
- **Palette:** Three modes keyed on `[data-mode]`:
  - Dark (default): near-black `#0a0a0a`, warm off-white `#f0ebe5`, blood-red accent `#c41e3a`.
  - Reader: warm cream `#f5efe6`, dark warm-gray text `#1a1816`, darker accent `#a91930`.
  - HC: pure black/white, lifted accent `#ff4060`, yellow focus ring `#ffeb3b`.
- **Future axis:** `data-palette="cb-*"` reserved for colorblind-safe variants (deutan/protan/tritan).
- **Influences:** Risograph print, zine culture, underground photography (Paul Clipson), analog/experimental.
- **Reject categorically:** SaaS landing-page aesthetics, pastels, gradients, drop shadows, rounded soft corners, Material Design, generic AI design.

Visual anchor reference: `alisendjsc-crypto.github.io/efilist-argument-library` (do NOT inherit its patterns — reconcile to the wuld.ink token system in session C).

- **THE v0_2 defect (root cause, confirmed at the source):** `layman_index_validator_v0_2.py` built its tier map as `{n["id"]: n.get("tier") for n in src["objections"]}` -- reading tier from the objections-index, whose record shape is `{gloss,id,keywords,title}` (tierless). So `tier.get(nid)` was always `None`, `== 4` never fired, and the T4 dissolve-defang gate was INERT against every real objections-index (the K194 finding). Dissolve-defang was hand-grep-enforced across all 5 wings (T4 count anthro 0 / veganism 1 / abortion 3 / RTD 7 / trans 1); the flagship's 31 T4 + 6 T5 = 37 dissolve nodes make hand-grep untenable.
- **THE FORK (`layman_index_validator_v0_3.py`, `acdbb1fe`/12,728, root; v0_2 KEPT in-tree, superseded pin):** (1) `field_bands` read from the layman index's own block (tolerant of `[lo,hi]` / `{min,max}` / `{lo,hi}`; fallback to the v0_2 defaults) -- no wing hardcode; all 5 landed indexes carry `field_bands` (regression prints `bands=index`). (2) tier read from a REQUIRED third arg `<tier-source-corpus>` via a container-agnostic `node_list` (handles `objections[]` [4 wings + flagship] AND `nodes[]` [veganism module]); tier coerced to `int`. The dissolve gate now fires on real ids. (3) TWO configurable sets replacing the hardcoded `== 4`: **`DISSOLVE_TIERS`** (hard-FAIL, default `{4}` = Genuine-Philosophical, the charter-defined dissolves-but-does-not-defeat / remains-standing / permission-win tier) + **`DISSOLVE_WARN_TIERS`** (soft-WARN, default `{5}` = Meta-Objection); both CLI-overridable (`--dissolve-tiers` / `--warn-tiers`); a tier in both is hard (dissolve wins). (4) NEW **tier-source COVERAGE gate**: every index id must resolve to a tier in the source, else FAIL -- a stale/wrong `--tier-source` can no longer silently disable the dissolve gate.
- **T5-META POLICY (the ONE design input, settled from the charter + flagship tiers-meta, RECOMMENDED not locked):** charter line 84 -- the inherited tier frame is *emotional/reflexive -> folk -> structural/pragmatic -> genuine-philosophical (T4) -> meta (T5)*; the flagship `tiers` meta names {1 Emotional/Reflexive, 2 Folk Philosophical, 3 Structural/Pragmatic, 4 Genuine Philosophical, 5 Meta-Objection}. **T4 dissolve-defang is CHARTER-CERTAIN** (K155/K146 Conceptual-Residue: a genuine commitment "remains standing" -> the win is on the permission axis, never defeat/route/remove). **T5-meta is NOT charter-guaranteed**: a category (the 6 flagship T5s = self-defeating / imposing-values / red-button-repugnant / meta-ethical-pluralism / heat-death-futility / moral-particularism) whose members may be answered by dissolve OR by legitimate refutation (self-defeating / red-button read like reductios one can defeat, not primitives one leaves standing). Scholar-prose grep is uninformative -- both T4 AND T5 scholar responses use defeat-vocab freely (the forbidden-vocab rule is a LAYMAN-register discipline, not a scholar claim). So default `{4}` hard + `{5}` warn: a T5 card carrying dissolve-vocab ALWAYS surfaces (WARN, forcing library-Claude's per-node read) but never silently ships, WITHOUT prejudging the meta tier. **Promote to `--dissolve-tiers 4,5` at the flagship fold ONLY if library-Claude confirms the 6 authored meta cards are uniformly dissolve-register.** Zero regression cost -- no wing has T5.

- **35% sfx default:** `DEFAULTS.sfx` 0 -> 0.35 (the live default was already `vfx:0.35`; only sfx moved). Fresh visitors (no stored prefs) now hear SFX at 35% after the first gesture; the slider still mutes at 0; existing stored prefs are respected (only new users get the floor). Popover copy rewritten off "default OFF".
- **Synced-smart boot power-on (AQ):** the flash used to fire on page-open while the boot sound waited for the first gesture (autoplay rule) -> unsynced. `boot()` now, when sfx>0, HOLDS the flash to land WITH `bootSeq()` on the first gesture (one power-on); when sound is muted it flashes immediately on open. `BOOT_KEY` is set only when the boot actually fires (so a land-then-navigate still boots on the page where the first gesture happens).
- **Generative ambiance engine:** a synth BED alternative to the YouTube playlist -- an evolving mechanical whir/hum drone (50/100/100.7/150Hz layered through a 0.13Hz-wobbled lowpass = the whir + faint band-passed air + a 0.06Hz breathing amp) + sparse haunting analog-piano motifs (C-minor-ish set, 3-partial soft voice, 4-11s gaps, occasional two-note fall) + occasional faint breath (band-passed noise swell, 16-42s gaps), all through the existing convolver room. A `youtube <-> generated` source toggle injected onto `.ambient-bar` (+ a "bed" popover row): "generated" pauses the playlist via `WuldAmbient.toggle()` (tracked `prefs.ambPausedYt`), rides the ambient volume (`ambLevel()`), persists via `prefs.amb` across navigation (the bed re-arms on the next page's first gesture; YT stays off via its own persisted `on:false`). NO edit to `ambient-player.js` -- coordinated only through its public `WuldAmbient` API, so no second `?v=` sweep.
- **VFX registry:** interaction one-shots routed parallel to the sound cues -- nav->`pulse` (accent tint, lightness held) / buttons->`flick` (CRT brightness) / typing->`glitch` (headings shiver) + `burst`/`roll`/`bloom` + `data-wh-vfx="X"` per element / `data-wh-vfx-scene` per page. A single `.wh-fx` stage (z 92, one effect at a time) + a `wh-glitch` heading kick; each peak swelled by `fxi() = 0.4 + 0.6*bleedNow()` (the same 3am bleed) -> barely-there by day, strong at 3am; legibility-safe (short, mix-blend holds lightness); fully off under `prefers-reduced-motion` (`.wh-fx{display:none}`). `data-wh="none"` mutes both sound + default vfx.

**K207 (2026-07-06, wrong-hour bed DECOUPLED + on-by-default + skip-auto-disable + new nav pluck; ambient-player paired-track default -- Cowork-native wuld build; NO PIN, NO canon, NO efilist):** Track (Josiah's K206 feedback: loves it; make the synth bed ON by default paired with a specific looping YT track [Das Buch der Klange Part 6], auto-disable the bed if the listener skips away, fix the dead-bar bug, and give nav links a more interesting sound). Two component edits (`wrong-hour.{css,js}` K206->K207 + `ambient-player.js` K30->K207) + a combined `?v` sweep; the mascot relay staged separately (`D:\mascot-synth-bed-relay.md`). **AQ: none this turn** (K206 feedback was directive; the one flag -- the site-wide ambient default change -- is surfaced to Josiah in the close, reversible).
- **THE DEAD-BAR BUG (root cause + fix):** clicking `[synth bed]` paused YouTube via `WuldAmbient.toggle()` -> ambient-player set `[data-state="off"]`, whose CSS sets `pointer-events:none` on every bar control except `#ambient-onoff`/`#ambient-dismiss` -> the injected `[fx]`/`[synth bed]` chips went dead until `[ambient on]` was clicked. FIX = DECOUPLE the bed from YouTube: the bed is now an INDEPENDENT layer that never pauses the playlist (they coexist). Plus a wrong-hour.css override (`.ambient-player[data-state="off"] .wh-chip, .wh-bedchip { pointer-events:auto !important; opacity:1 !important }`) so the effect chips stay live even if the listener turns ambient fully off.
- **Bed ON by default:** `DEFAULTS` drops `amb`/`ambPausedYt`, gains `bedOn: true` (fresh + K206 users default to bed-on; a user who toggles it off in K207 is respected via `typeof r.bedOn === "boolean"`). The toggle is a plain on/off (`ambSetBed`/`ambToggleBed`); the chip reads `[synth bed]` with aria-pressed driving the accent (bed on = red). `ambPauseYt`/`ambResumeYt`/`ambSetSource`/`ambToggleSource` REMOVED (grep 0); public API `ambience`->`bed`.
- **Auto-disable on skip:** a 2s watch (`ambStartWatch`) reads `WuldAmbient.getState().currentVideoId`, baselines the first non-null (the opening/paired track), and calls `ambSetBed(false)` when it changes -- a skip (or a natural advance when loop is off) turns the bed off until manual re-enable. Loop-one keeps the paired track looping, so a fresh visitor's bed stays put (a loop re-seeks the same video -> currentVideoId stable).
- **Paired-track default (ambient-player.js):** `DEFAULT_STATE.shuffleOn` true->false + `loopOne` false->true -> a FRESH visitor opens on playlist track 1, looped. Josiah reorders the playlist so *Das Buch der Klange Part 6* is track 1. Existing listeners keep their persisted shuffle/loop (the readState merge-over-DEFAULT_STATE contract). SITE-WIDE default change, flagged to Josiah, reversible.
- **New nav sound (`soft`):** the K205 felt-tap replaced by a warm resonant pluck -- triangle through a resonant lowpass that snaps closed (the pluck) + a sub-octave for warmth + a faint octave-up shimmer, pitch walking a minor-pentatonic set (A C D E G, `NAV_HZ`) so consecutive nav clicks form a little melody. Still soft (peak ~0.26), through the convolver room.

(b) **soft reworked** -> a warm, muted analog pluck: sawtooth through a resonant lowpass that opens modestly (hz*3.4) then closes near the fundamental (hz*1.05) = warm / never-bright, + a sub-octave body; the bright octave-up SHIMMER REMOVED (that was the shared "chime" element). (c) **WORD_HZ** [523.25..880] -> [392..659.25] (down a fourth: G4 A4 B4 D5 E5 -- "a fraction of an octave lower"); the wordChime character (triangle + shimmer) otherwise held. - **THE DESIGN — donor-chrome GRAFT, not an embedded template:** a new page's chrome (head/OG/JSON-LD scaffold, nav, footer, component `?v=` includes, inline styles) is grafted at op time from a LIVE donor page the ?v sweeps maintain (blog donor /blog/the-easiest-case/; essay donor /essays/architecture-of-moral-disaster/) -> pages are born chrome-current FOREVER, where an embedded template would be born stale after the first post-embed sweep (sweeps replace the CURRENT ?v literal; a template fork inside the Worker would silently miss every future sweep — the trap that motivated the design). Every donor substitution is occurrence-COUNTED (descs replaced FIRST — the AMD desc embeds its own title; then title x5, url x4, datePublished/Modified x1 each; then the whole <main> region swapped for generated content); ANY count mismatch = 422 `op_refused` "donor drift" at preview — fails loud, never lands half-grafted bytes. Belt: post-graft bare-donor-slug leak scan + whole-page tag balance (open==close per SITE_TAGS) + index tag-delta == {}.
- **blog-post (UI section 11):** title / slug (blank = derived; donor-slug-containment refused) / date (client-side default = operator-LOCAL today — no UTC skew class) / optional Source meta line / summary (meta description x4 AND the card excerpt) / optional figure (URL[/assets/… or https://…] + required alt + optional caption) / body (md-lite: blank-line paragraphs, **bold**, *italic*, [links](url); escape-then-md so raw HTML never passes) / position (blank = NEWEST-FIRST via a clean post-list-anchor insert that preserves the card rhythm; 0 = append; N = ops.py slot semantics). Creates `src/blog/<slug>/index.html` + prepends the /blog/ card; BlogPosting JSON-LD carries `<date>T09:00:00-07:00`.
- **essay-page (UI section 12):** title / slug / date / genre (page eyebrow `Essay  /  <genre>` + card eyebrow) / summary / optional audio_duration (M:SS or H:MM:SS -> the audio-intro band wired `essays/<slug>/full.mp3`; the mp3 itself stays the operator's R2 drag-drop lane, K3 class) / optional reading (blank = auto ~words/220 min; omitted when shell) / body (`## Heading` lines open numbered sections -> Section I/II/… eyebrows via the EXISTING gallery `romanize`; EMPTY body = placeholder shell for chat-side fill) / position (blank = APPEND — the essays list reads as a sequence). Mirrors the live essays byte-for-byte in apparatus: `data-readable="<slug>"`, mode-toggle (dark/reader/hc), mag-slider, essay.css + audio-player + mode-toggle + yurei chrome riding the donor. Card half REUSES `siteEssayCard`; card tag uses a REAL middot (NEW K212 lesson: `siteEssayCard` siteEsc's the tag, so an `&middot;` entity would land double-escaped `&amp;middot;` — pass real UTF-8 `·` through siteEsc instead).
- **Machinery:** both ops are `kind:"multi"` (the cache-bump class) via a shared runner `siteRunPagePlusCard`: preview = ghHead + donor + index + page-exists gate (404 required; 5 subrequests), commit re-runs deterministically at the CAS'd head -> `ghMultiCommit` (ONE commit `site-admin: <op> — <title> (/<section>/<slug>/)`, ref CAS force:false -> 409 on race).
